for the battery only at filtered 3.4 V / 5 %. F52/F53 (`network.py`, `connectivity.py`): WRONG_KEY latches on the 2nd, saved-network reselect after 30 s DISCONNECTED, interface re-add, `wifi-journal.jsonl`. F17: no DCS reads while reads are doubled, DSI restart ≤ 2 when no frames, splash CRTC cycle. F23/F34: init waits for both partitions; recovery watchdog from the first step. F56/F57: `/sys/firmware/y2_pmic/power_status` → `previous-boot-evidence.json``pmic` (on 162: long-press reset enabled, 11 s, no record); pstore panic re-send fixed.

  - F47/F48 sleep (earlier today): Linux `d3b49f2` (UART0 RX pad to GPIO in sleep, UART-busy dump + one retry),

`2200bad` (a refused sleep stays dark); Reborn `57b47b2` (screen off at once on Power), `9ee61f0` (no Sleep page). Identity `df66c7e`/`c5029b1` (8.42, seq 10800) and `5c0bc11` (seq 10810). Test build 160 ran on the owner's player; 161 (seq 10810, Reborn `9ee61f0`) is built in `out/release-161`, its device check is not recorded.
  - F49 large SD card (Linux `84e5366`): report `d32f8f3c` (install `b4bc0c95`; the user wrote "BFBC0C95"), 8.41,

1 TB exFAT, 71 400 songs. `fsck.exfat -n` took 58.8–59.6 s; above ≈72 000 songs it hit the fixed 60 s, the card went read-only (`check_timed_out`) and Repair (`fsck.exfat -p`, 60.5 s, nothing changed) could not help. Now: admission check `ADMISSION_CHECK_SECONDS` 20 (ext4 `EXT4_CHECK_SECONDS` 60, no background path); a timeout mounts read-only as `read_only: checking` (observe: Ready, not writable; `write_window` → `card_being_checked`; not repairable); `service.media_verify` starts `y2-platform media verify` (retry `VERIFY_RETRY_S` 30) → `media.verify`: checker with `CHECK_SECONDS` 1800, no lock while reading, applies only to the same mount + card (identity id + diskseq), a failed check across a sleep (`CLOCK_BOOTTIME` − monotonic grew) gives no verdict. Pass → `protected` (USB window opens on its next 10-s tick); finding/timeout → `filesystem_errors`/`check_timed_out` (Repair). `sd-admissions.json` rows carry `background: true`. fsck.fat/fsck.exfat `-n` open the device O_RDONLY without a mount check (dosfstools 4.2, exfatprogs 1.4.3 sources in `out/build/buildroot/build`). Tests: `test_platform_storage_tools` (4 new), `test_platform_update.test_media_verify_*`. Docs: [STORAGE](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2FSTORAGE.md%23the-sd-card&anchor=the-sd-card).
  - Quick scan (Reborn `c2ef3af`): the same report's restart scans took 62–70 s to list 70 500 files. Automatic

scans (`Scanner::scan` = `ScanMode::Quick`: start, storage change, after USB) skip a folder whose mtime is unchanged while the storage's free space (`quick_fingerprint`: mountinfo type exfat or ext4 only, `reborn_platform::free_bytes`) equals the last complete walk. Records: additive tables `scan_folders`/`scan_volumes` (with `METADATA_REVISION`), written only after a complete verified walk, cleared otherwise. Skipped folders' rows are marked seen (`seen_folders`, range on UNIQUE(source_id,path)), counts/playlists/subfolders come from the record. Scan for Music and control `scan` = `scan_all` (`ScanMode::Walk`). Trade-off: an edit on a computer that changes neither a folder's time nor the free space (most tag edits) shows only after Scan for Music. Kernel facts: 6.18 `fs/exfat/super.c` counts used clusters from the bitmap at mount; `namei.c` sets the parent's mtime on create/unlink/rename. Tests: `quick_scan_tests` (4); workspace tests, strict Clippy, fmt pass.

Also: sleep during the check, card pulled during the check.

fields (`quick`, `folders_skipped`, `elapsed_ms`); copy a song over USB (expect a full walk, `quick: false`); Settings → Scan for Music (full walk).

state + readme). Publish only when the owner says so.

Draft reply for `d32f8f3c`: "Thanks, your report showed it exactly. Your card isn't damaged: the player checks a card before using it, and with about 71 000 songs on your 1 TB card that check takes just under a minute, the time limit it had. Above about 72 000 songs it ran out of time, so the player kept the card read-only and offered Repair, which found nothing to fix. The next version uses the card straight away and finishes the check in the background, and after a restart it no longer reads every unchanged folder again. If you edit tags on a computer, use Settings → Scan for Music afterwards. Until then your music still plays; to add songs, put the card in your PC."

How-to reminders (details in memory notes): release = `release.py bump --version X --sequence N` → commit both repos (Reborn with `GIT_AUTHOR_*`/`GIT_COMMITTER_*` env, never model attribution) → `release.py build --label N` (tree must stay clean while it runs) → install on the player (`scp out/release-N/ota/*` to `/data/updates/owner-N`, `y2-platform update stage --package DIR`, `update apply`) → docs → `release.py publish --label N --title … --notes out/release-N-notes.md --yes` (Reborn HEAD must be the built commit: Reborn docs commit after publishing). Host checks: `tools/development/host-tests.sh` (906; the report tests are load-flaky, rerun alone), Reborn `cargo test --workspace`,`clippy -D warnings`,`fmt --check`, previews`cargo run --bin reborn-preview -- DIR` + `tools/preview/render_previews.py DIR`. Player SSH over USB (`root@10.42.0.1`, key `~/.ssh/y2linux_ed25519`); Wi-Fi may be idle-powered-down while dark (unblock the wlan rfkill); a background logger needs a long runtime when the owner unplugs (the card is always rw while USB is connected).

Previous entry: START HERE (2026-10-24): #52/#54 ARE SOLVED ON BETA 8.25 — REVIEW THE OTHER PENDING REPORTS, THEN TRIM. 2026-10-23: both reporters closed their issues. #54's 8.25 report (5f909e65, install 0400eb78): `tries=1 ready_ms=30 errors=0 cycles=0 stream_cycles=0`— the chip-powered start (`PWDN_CTL=0x10`) alone fixed a unit whose crystal failed at every power-up on 8.22/8.24; 23 tracks, 0 playback errors. #52 (2813306c) confirmed in text, no report. Ledger rows added. Nothing of 8.25's retry machinery was exercised, so the 8.24 trim plan (keep stock-equivalent parts, remove non-stock crystal code; keep the switches as diagnostics) may start. Before that: 12 other pending reports from 2026-10-22/23 (installs 00322807, 03df8d42, 07e62fce, 432e74ab, 4ff97dff, 703f1fbf, 72f7e3ee ×3, cc1fe289, 09cabe08, aa96e00a) are unreviewed — ledger first. Previous entry: START HERE (2026-10-24): AFTER BETA 8.25 (BASELINE 124) — READ THE #52/#54 RESULTS. Published 2026-10-23 (seq 8901, Linux build `f7f23d4`; Reborn `40386bc`); the owner's player runs it. 8.24 did not fix #52, and #54 fails the same way: the CS43131 crystal flags XTAL_ERR at power-up (both 8.24 reports, `cycles=0`). Full evidence, stock RE and Fable's analysis: `docs/planning/ISSUE-52-54-CRYSTAL-HANDOFF.md`. 8.25: crystal start with the chip powered as stock except the headphone amplifier (with it, static at screen wake on the owner's player), three tries with 500 ms rail cycles, one cold start for the first song; switches `y2_xtal_full_power`, `y2_xtal_start_hp`, `y2_xtal_quiet_start`, `echo cycle > y2_hp/xtal`; `y2_hp/xtal` adds `tries= start_ms= stream_cycles=`. Order: (1) reports (`fetch.py --pending`, ledger): `tries=`, `errors=`, `stream_cycles=`, `unconfirmed=` on 2813306c and 0400eb78, and any test output in the issue comments. (2) If tries>1 then ready: the retries work; if ready only with `start_hp=1`, design a click-free amplifier start. If still never ready: supply margin (VGP2 under load, real GPIO rail voltages) is next, not the register sequence. (3) The trim plan of the 8.24 entry waits until #52/#54 play. Next free label 125, sequence above 8901. Report BETA-8.25.

Previous entry: START HERE (2026-10-24): AFTER BETA 8.24 (BASELINE 123) — WAIT FOR #52/#54, THEN TRIM THE CRYSTAL CODE. Published 2026-10-23 (seq 8800, Linux build `2a21690`, docs `6cc7eb2`; Reborn `e199d9c`, docs `db4c407`); the owner's player runs it. #52 cause: every failure on 8.21–8.23 was a 48 kHz start through the CS43131 PLL (PLL_READY -110, then XTAL_ERR); stock `audio_policy.conf` output = 44100 only, so stock never runs the PLL. 8.24: AFE 44.1 kHz only, codec refuses non-crystal rates, Reborn `reborn_audio::WIRED_RATE` (decoder converts; one open sink for all rates), codec power cycle after a crystal error (`y2_hp/xtal``cycles=`), cold start at probe when rails are on (`cold_start=`). Device: 13/13 (48/96 kHz at 44100 Hz, crystal selected, 50 reuses, 0 reopens). Correction: a software or OTA restart already resets VGP2 (2.5 V reset value, off at probe), so the "VGP2 survives a restart" idea in `f66d71d` was wrong; the code comment is fixed on main (`0da8393`, unbuilt). Order for today:
  - Reports first (`UserReports/REVIEWED.md`, `tools/reports/fetch.py --pending`). #52 (2813306c), #54 (0400eb78) on

8.24: `y2_hp/xtal` (`ready_ms`, `errors`, `unconfirmed`, `cycles`, `cold_start`), any `refused: it needs the PLL` or `codec power-cycled` kernel line, `sink_reused`/`sink_reopened`. #54 failed differently on 8.22 (crystal not ready 45.9 s after power-up), so the 44.1 kHz conversion alone may not fix it.
  - GitHub: no comment on #52 about 8.24 yet (owner not asked; offered). #56 comment's battery sentence still overstated.
  - Owner decision recorded 2026-10-23: no rollback of the crystal changes now. Most of 8.21–8.23 copies stock

(crystal with the codec, never switched, stock power-up, hold while the screen is on); the pre-8.21 behaviour was the unusual one, and which change fixed #52 is unknown. Once #52/#54 confirm 8.24, trim only non-stock parts that prove unnecessary: (a) probe cold start (proven not to trigger) first, (b) the power cycle if no report shows `cycles>0`, (c) the up-to-1-s ready wait only after reports settle (it gives `ready_ms`). Measure the hold's battery cost before touching it (a shorter idle delay is the milder lever).
  - Still owed on the owner's player: click check by ear at track changes, unplug USB → real sleep/wake → play on

headphones, full power-off/on, overnight drain vs 8.22.
  - Open questions unchanged: #56 drain in a frozen recovery on a charger, F34's cause, the synchronous hold write at

screen wake (≈70 ms when the codec was down), car AVRCP metadata, long-book seek, #42, #45, #47, F23.

Next free label 124, sequence above 8800. Report BETA-8.24.

Previous entry: Y2LINUX 1.0 BETA 8.21 (BASELINE 120) PUBLISHED — CRYSTAL RUN AS STOCK (#52, #54). Installed on the owner's player before publishing (build 120, seq 8500, Linux `2e914d6`, Reborn `cdc6b43`). 8.20's 15 µA boost failed on #52, and #54 (install 0400eb78) is a second player that never plays on headphones but does on stock. Stock `cs43131_hw_init` powers the crystal with the codec and masks/never reads XTAL_RDY/XTAL_ERR; we had started it only at hw_params and stopped it before every retry (≤0.5 s continuous). Overlay 0030 (Linux `9ba00b9`): crystal on at runtime resume, off only at runtime suspend, a stream waits until it ran 1 s then uses it anyway, later flags only counted, no auto boost/rail cycle, codec autosuspend 5 s; `y2_hp/xtal``running= ready= unconfirmed= errors= boosted=`. Device runs all pass (report BETA-8.21); the slow path itself is owed by the #52/#54 reporters. #53 (clock = build time without Wi-Fi) diagnosed, fix awaits the owner's choice. Ledger rows in `UserReports/REVIEWED.md`. Next free label 121, sequence above 8500.

Previous entry: Y2LINUX 1.0 BETA 8.20 (BASELINE 119) PUBLISHED — #52 HEADPHONE CRYSTAL. Installed on the owner's player before publishing (build 119, seq 8400, Linux `0f0b4ed`, Reborn `cdae78e`). #52 = the #48/#51 player (install 2813306c): its CS43131 flags XTAL_ERR at the stock 12.5 µA bias; overlay 0030 now raises the bias to 15 µA after a failed start or a stop while running (`y2_hp/xtal``boosted=`, writable `boost`), Reborn's open deadline 5 s, reports collect `y2_hp/xtal`. Device runs at stock and forced 15 µA all pass (report BETA-8.20). Owed: the reporter's confirmation (next report's `y2_hp/xtal`). Test tones (48/96 kHz) on the owner's Internal Storage `Reborn Test 48k/`. 8 uploaded reports still pending review (`fetch.py --pending`). Next free label 120, sequence above 8400.

Previous entry: Y2LINUX 1.0 BETA 8.11 (BASELINE 106) PUBLISHED — INTERNAL STORAGE SETUP ON A LOW BATTERY. User report `UserReports/y2linux-report-20261019-153610` ("internal storage is still 700 mb after multiple restarts"): setup `Pending`/`battery_low` at every start (3.54–3.59 V under boot load < 3.7 V), and Reborn said "Restart to finish setting up" for every Pending. Fixed: 25 % from BAT0 `voltage_ocv` via the stock profile (Linux `fee6b36`), Reborn shows Connect a charger and restart (`f649609`). Report `docs/validation/Y2-LINUX-1.0-BETA-8.11.md`, audit BETA-8.11. Release `1.0.0-beta.8.11`, sequence 7500, Linux `9c7f079`, Reborn `727fd47`; not installed on a player. Previous entry: Y2LINUX 1.0 BETA 8.10 (BASELINE 105) PUBLISHED — CHARGING FIX #47, ERROR REPORTS OVER WI-FI. Owner: "publish without it and answer the issue without it" (no wall-charger test on 105). Release `1.0.0-beta.8.10`, sequence 7400, Linux `9df5ccf`, Reborn `cfac12b`. #47 cause: `y2_charge_prepare` refused any inherited CV other than selector 30/0; the reporter's PMIC held 31 (4.1875 V) → fault 0x80/-EIO on every charger. Now CV/input OVP/battery OVP are replaced by stock values and read back; only an asserted battery-OVP detector refuses; refusals named in `check_*`. Reply posted on #47; waiting for their confirmation. Uploads live: players are asked once; reports in R2, `tools/reports/fetch.py`. Owed: Cloudflare WAF rate-limit rule + R2 billing alert (owner), wall-charger check on 8.10, progress page by eye. Flaky host tests seen once each: `test_error_reports` queue/save (only inside the full suite). Next free label 106, sequence above 7400.

Report `docs/validation/Y2-LINUX-1.0-BETA-8.7.md`, audit BETA-8.7; release `1.0.0-beta.8.7`, sequence 6900, Linux `f7b4fb5`, Reborn `82fe089`. Owner installs by OTA (check: panel path unchanged, `panel=st7701s` or record). Replies posted on #45/#46 (test steps, LK hash, jack answer). Next free label 96, sequence above 6900.

Previous entry: #45/#46 SECOND-PLAYER INVESTIGATION — PARSER FIX COMMITTED, UNRELEASED. REA fm-lk 0x81e0af60: LK's list is `[gc9503v, st7701]` (0x81e3821c); the `lcm=` name is the first driver whose compare_id matched, else the last (st7701); the digit is a separate DCS 0x0A read. GC9503V compare (0x81e1eb60) only matches DCS 0x04 = xx 95 03/04, so `lcm=0-gc9503v…` (samanz's boots) is a positive GC9503V ID that boot-policy.h ignored. Fix: gc9503v with either digit, st7701 still only with `lcm=1-`; tests `test_power_policy` (mutation caught), `test_power_final_27`; boot.o/panel.o W=1 clean; host 805 OK; 8.1 report row corrected. GC9503V power-down stays opt-in (CONFIRMED_PANELS). Not done on purpose: "no power-down on a lag boot" — samanz's 7.10 run showed the lag clears after the first screen-off (DIS domain power cycle). Hardware comparison (owner vs samanz): identical except panel, eMMC model (FN62MB 2019 vs FNX2MB 2017, same CSD) and his 3.2.1 bootloaders (his LK hash unknown). Replies on #45/#46 drafted, not posted (owner's OK). Ships with the next release.

Previous entry: START HERE (2026-10-18): Y2LINUX 1.0 BETA 8.6 (BASELINE 94) PUBLISHED — AUDIO PLAYBACK REVIEW. Owner: "publish as a new update, once you deem this good". Report `docs/validation/Y2-LINUX-1.0-BETA-8.6.md`, audits AUDIO-REVIEW-8.5 and BETA-8.6; the owner's player runs test build 94 = this release (`/data/ota-94` deleted). Next free label 95, sequence above 6800. Owed: battery current with a profile (USB unplugged), DAC Filter by ear, sleep with a level, the output measurements of HEADPHONE-OUTPUT.md. History of the work: Owner asked for a review of all audio playback, then "do it all", then "do the test on the player".
  - Test build 94: Linux `fd32cdb`, Reborn `cefcc8a`, `release.py build --label 94` (148 s, container suite 778,

no firmware); stage + apply over SSH (owner approved each step in /permissions), install ≈16 s, Acknowledged 6800. Pre-existing, unrelated: a Python `OSError: [Errno 22]` line in kmsg at ≈29 s on every boot since 8.5.
  - **Device results (buttons pressed over SSH by writing evdev records to the real input nodes — no uinput needed;

screen via `/data/system/capture/y2-screen-stream`; a short Power press wakes a dark screen, Select/Back/wheel are ignored while dark):** profile list K→M has no "LICENSE-AutoEq"; profile off while playing → `dsp_rise_fade` 2000 ms, back on → no fade (preamp −6.1 for AirPods Pro, P1); EQ change in a held pause → `pause_released`; pressing the checked level → no restart; High while playing → `level_raise_fade`, Next 0.7 s later → `fade_continued` 907 ms (no jump); High = 0x30/0x1E Live High, Low = 0x10/0x1C, Master −19.5 dB, 0 xruns; `hpload_measure` during playback → EBUSY. No kernel warnings.
  - CPU/battery (60 s, owner's AirPods Pro profile, same song, `python3 measure.py` with cpufreq time_in_state):

8.5: Reborn 17.9 % of one core (playback thread 12.0 %), CPU floor 747.5 MHz the whole time, SLIDLE/DORMANT 0, WFI entries 68 248/min. 8.6: floor 598 MHz (avg 608 MHz), Reborn ≈118 MHz-equivalent (playback ≈75 MHz vs ≥90 on 8.5, ≥16 % fewer cycles), WFI entries 30 443/min (fewer wake-ups); profile off: playback 4.6 % ≈29 MHz, so a 10-band profile still costs ≈46 MHz of the decode thread. Deep idle stays 0 during any playback (the INFRA-audio rule, see the awake-idle analysis), so D1 saves the frequency floor, not idle states. mA not measured (USB on).
  - Side effects: test playback scrobbled one song to the owner's Last.fm. Restored afterwards: track 500 at

1:21.7 paused, volume 47 (30 during the level tests), Low, Fast, AirPods Pro, Normalize Track. `/data/ota-94` (59 MB copy of the package) left on the player.
  - Not checked on the player: volume up during a fade (unit test only), DAC Filter click (listening), sleep

with a level (USB connected), the A1 race itself (not triggerable on purpose).
  - Earlier, host stage of the same work: Audit AUDIO-REVIEW-8.5; Reborn `0b55cbb` (+ docs `86b7baa`), state "Unreleased —

audio playback review". Nothing was built for the player, written to it or published.
  - Kernel (0030, Linux): the level's PDN_HP check and write now run under the card's DAPM mutex (before, a

stream starting between check and write could get OUT_FS written with the amplifier on); `hpload_measure` refuses an open stream and holds DAPM while measuring. `tests/test_hp_level.py` models the lock (mutations caught); W=1 compile in the sandbox clean; host suite 805 OK.
  - Reborn: fade carries over a reopened sink and through volume up; louder sound settings fade in

(`dsp_rise_fades`); held pause released on every sound change; same Headphone Level = no restart; EQ no longer PlaybackHeavy; one FFmpeg graph thread (host −43 %/−20 % CPU, bit-identical); preamp over 44.1–384 kHz (six shipped profiles 0.1–0.4 dB lower); license file not a profile; FFI layout asserts, new loud fixture, clip test at S24/S16. 539 tests, fmt, strict Clippy.
  - Answers: OUT_FS was only ever written live through the two races above. Clip safety holds with ReplayGain,

crossfade (tri curves sum ≤ limit), gapless (alimiter latency=1 trims/pads) and 24-bit; files below 44.1 kHz rely on the limiter (FFmpeg bypasses filters above Nyquist). Old Reborn + new kernel: 4.8 dB quieter; new Reborn + old kernel: as 8.4. A reboot with High saved plays High without a fade (by design).
  - Owed on the player (buttons, after a test build with the owner's OK): Headphone Profile shows 47 entries and no

"LICENSE-AutoEq"; raise the level while playing, press Next within 2 s: the fade continues; volume up during the fade: no jump; profile on → off while playing: fades in; pause until held, Reset Settings at High, Play: Low; press the checked level: no restart; DAC Filter change while playing: listen for a click. Measure CPU (decode+filter threads, HD 650) and the playback current with a profile vs 8.5 (PlaybackNormal now).
  - Not done on purpose: refusing files whose preamp is far below what their filters need (the fade covers the

jump); covering sample rates below 44.1 kHz in the preamp (would lower many profiles by up to 6 dB).

Previous entry: **START HERE (2026-10-18): Y2LINUX 1.0 BETA 8.5 (BASELINE 93) PUBLISHED — HEADPHONE LEVEL, HEADPHONE PROFILES (AUTOEQ, 47 SHIPPED), DAC FILTER, SOUND ON ONE SCREEN.** Owner: "publish it as beta 8.5". Report `docs/validation/Y2-LINUX-1.0-BETA-8.5.md`, audit BETA-8.5; the owner's player runs test build 93 = this release. History of the work below (first written when it was host-tested only). Owner question from an email (stock bass roll-off, "output gain switching with impedance"). Guide + measurement plan: [HEADPHONE-OUTPUT](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2FHEADPHONE-OUTPUT.md); audit HP-LEVEL-EQ.
  - Code: Linux `2c59a31` (0030: ALSA `HP Output Level` Low/Medium/High = OUT_FS 0x10/0x20/0x30 with hv_seq's

HV_EN, written only while PDN_HP is set (now or at the HiFi DAC PRE_PMU), `HP Output Level Live`, probe default Low; `tests/test_hp_level.py` C harness, mutation-checked; FFmpeg `lowshelf highshelf`; `tools/audio/test_signals.py`). Reborn `dc66880` (Settings → Sound: Headphone Level, DAC Filter; Equalizer → Headphone Profile from `EQ` folders, 10 filters, clip-safe preamp; Master −19.5 dB with the control, −24 dB without; 2-s fade after a rise). Owner choice: 10 bands (AutoEQ files have 10). Host: Linux 804 OK (host-tests.sh), driver compiles W=1 in the sandbox; Reborn 529 tests, fmt, strict Clippy.
  - Facts found: DS1155F2 OUT_FS 00/01/10/11 = 0.5/1.0/1.41/1.73 Vrms; HV_EN = 1 specified for ≥ 600 Ω and

VP ≥ 3.3 V (VGP2 in the DT is 1.8 V, so VP is wired elsewhere, probably to the battery: unknown). Stock never writes CLASS_H (runs 0x10 + 0x1E) and writes OUT_FS with the amplifier on. Y2Linux full volume was ≈0.11 Vrms (1.73 V −24 dB); stock's top step ≈0.71 Vrms. `c852008` (Baseline 49) already had `y2_hp/hp_gain` + on-demand load measurement, unused by Reborn; the 102-Ω board offset found then may be real series resistance (output impedance), so measure it.
  - **Test build 91 = 1.0.0-beta.8.5, sequence 6750 (Linux `5de9c95`, Reborn `14aad05`), installed 2026-10-18 over SSH

(stage + apply, install 16.0 s, Acknowledged).** `5de9c95` makes a resumed build rebuild FFmpeg when its options change (it never compiled the new filters before). Device results: probe default Low (HP_OUT_CTL_1 0x10, CLASS_H 0x1C; 8.4 had 0x30/0x1E); wired play: PWDN 0xA6, 0x10/0x1C, Master −19.5 dB, `hp_level_live` 0; High requested during playback left the registers alone, a silent stream afterwards came up at 0x30/0x1E (Live High), Low while down written at once. Runtime FFmpeg lists lowshelf/highshelf. Session-edit tests (owner granted stopping Reborn; backup `/data/reborn/state/session.json.before-hp-test`, restored after): High + Slow Phase Compensated + HD 650 profile → startup wrote 0x30/0x1E and PCM_FILT_OPT 0xC2, play Live High, Master −19.5, no fade (startup baseline), graph `EQ=configured,preamp=-6.4dB,alimiter` (−6.1 sanitized), 0 xruns/filter/decode errors. CPU on the same song: decode+filter threads 4.4 % → 11.7 % of one core with the 10-band profile (second thread = the EQ slice thread; one thread may be cheaper). Original settings back (Low, Fast, no profile), player paused. Not run: UI changes (no input injection), the fade after a chosen rise, profile scan/import screens, system sleep (USB unplugged). Test files in `/media/internal/music/EQ/` (HD 650 + a broken one).
  - Test build 92 = sequence 6751 (Linux `ef1619c`, Reborn `770a6de`), installed 2026-10-18, Acknowledged: owner

disliked scrolling in Sound and chose layout C: Sound = Equalizer, Normalize, Gapless, Crossfade + Output card ("Level Low · Filter Fast"); the card opens `Screen::Output` (outputs checked + Headphone Jack group with Level and DAC Filter). Owner to try with the buttons.
  - Test build 93 = sequence 6752 (Linux `04e2dc8`, Reborn `b694670`), Acknowledged: 47 AutoEq profiles of common

headphones/IEMs ship in `/usr/share/reborn/eq` (Reborn `assets/eq`, pinned AutoEq `7ae0f56`, MIT license in legal-info); a storage `EQ` file replaces a shipped one by name. Owner tested the broken-file refusal ("it works"); the test copies on Internal Storage were removed (identical to the shipped ones).

debugfs HP_OUT_CTL_1/CLASS_H per level, the 2-s fade, listening at Low (should match the old loudness), suspend and resume keep the level, a profile file from Internal Storage `EQ/`. Then the emailer's measurements (plan in the guide; signal in `out/audio-test-signals/`, or regenerate). Not decided: the release number; attaching the test signal to a release.

Previous entry: START HERE (2026-10-18): Y2LINUX 1.0 BETA 8.4 (BASELINE 90) PUBLISHED — DARK DISPLAY AFTER A RESTART. Owner: "publish, I do OTA"; report `docs/validation/Y2-LINUX-1.0-BETA-8.4.md`, audit BETA-8.4. The 8.3 OTA's restart from the recovery came up dark and the health check rolled the install back. Logs (`out/dsi-lag-2026-10-18/`, local): on the dark boot mm-clocks took no`holding SMI common/larb0` (the SMI common gate was already closed at 0.36 s, before Lima), the handover logged `SMI gates 1 left closed by a restart` with larb0 `stat=1 greq=1`, and the DSI register reads lagged from the first modeset (3 power cycles useless): the leftover-gate case of POWER-FINAL-40 (watchdog restart keeps `MMSYS_CG_CON0`). The three lit restarts that day all took the hold. 42's restart handler (reopen SMI common/larb0 as the last step before the watchdog) was removed in 43 for a different cause; Linux `7ed664b` brings it back next to 43's hold and logs `SMI common gated before Linux touched it: CG_CON0=…` when the gate is found closed at probe. Not established: why only some restarts close the gate. Candidate 1.0.0-beta.8.4, sequence 6700, Linux `c339919`, Reborn identity only; container suite 775 OK. Owed: after the owner's OTA, check the 8.4 boot for `holding SMI common/larb0` and no `SMI gates … left closed` (`dmesg`, `/data/logs/system.log`), and the install `timings`; later restarts the same.

Previous entry: START HERE (2026-10-17, for the next session): FASTER UPLOADS — MULTI-PACKET (MODE 1) RECEIVE DMA. Owner: "i would like to do multi packet and fix the kernel instead" and continue in a fresh session. Plan [USB-RX-DMA-MODE1-PLAN](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fplanning%2FUSB-RX-DMA-MODE1-PLAN.md), audit row USB-RX-DMA (commit `39cb314`). **Plan written, not yet approved in detail, nothing built.**
  - Released state: Y2Linux 1.0 Beta 7.10 (Baseline 73, sequence 6300, tag `baseline-73`, Linux `72a565d` + docs, Reborn

`511dbf0` + docs) is GitHub latest and installed on the owner's player (confirmed 2026-10-17 00:40). 7.10 = musb zero-length-packet oops fix (`da0917d`, `kernel/patches/0065-y2-musb-pio-progress.patch`) + MoveObject/CopyObject (`4c0a54d`, `buildroot/package/y2-mtp/0004-y2-mtp-move-and-copy-objects.patch`, `y2_copy_file` in `tools/usb/mtp-safe.c`). `tools/usb/mtp-file-ops.c` (libmtp) passed all 26 steps on the player. 7.9's automatic Internal Storage passed on the player (509 kept files hash-identical). User guide for storage: `docs/STORAGE.md`.
  - Measured (owner's player, 7.10, `tools/usb/mtpbench.c`, libusb, SHA-256): download 37.7 MB/s (TX DMA), upload

8.7–10.3 MB/s on Internal Storage and SD. `/sys/devices/platform/11200000.usb/status`: `dma_rx_programs=0`, `pio_rx_bytes>0` → every received packet is PIO. Upload CPU: the musb IRQ (213) core is 96–100 % busy at ≈21–24 k IRQ/s whichever core it runs on (live affinity test on CPU3, restored to `f`); ≈45 µs CPU per 512-byte packet = the ceiling. Storage write ceilings (candidate 11): eMMC 30 MiB/s, SD 26 MiB/s. Double-buffered FIFOs and `ioread32_rep` are already in place.
  - Why RX DMA is off:`kernel/platform/usb.c``y2_dma_is_compatible()` keeps bulk-OUT unmapped unless sysfs `rx_dma=1`

(mode 0: two IRQs per packet, CPU-FIX02 storm guard, a lost ACKed packet after an IN completion within µs; FIX03 corrected the guard). Upstream mode 1 lives in `rxstate()` (patched overlay `.cache/overlays/647766a6…`, ≈lines 547–820) only for `short_not_ok` (mass storage): MTP needs short-packet/ZLP termination (abort channel, read moved bytes, PIO the short packet, give back the exact length).
  - First steps: (1) REA on the stock kernel `out/rea/fm-kernel.elf` (local, not in git): how MediaTek's musb driver

programmed RX DMA on this controller (mode, RXCSR/DMAREQ sequence, short-packet handling, errata). (2) Read `drivers/usb/musb/musbhsdma.c` (+ overlay 0055) and `musb_g_rx()` for the DMA-busy/short-packet interrupt path. (3) Implement mode 1 behind `rx_dma=2`, default off, keeping the receive event ring/counters; zero-fuzz overlay patch + manifest hash (see how 0065 was regenerated with `diff -u` from `.cache/sources/linux-6.18`). (4) Release with the switch off, owner installs, live A/B `echo 2 > /sys/devices/platform/11200000.usb/rx_dma` (a runtime write the owner must authorise), run the device tests of the plan, then default on in a later release.
  - Measuring: `cc -O2 -Wall -o /tmp/mtpbench tools/usb/mtpbench.c -lusb-1.0 -lcrypto && /tmp/mtpbench -s 0xffff0001 up

64 x.bin`(SD`0xffff0002`; the`clean` command in its usage text does not exist — it deletes its own files). Functional suite: `cc -O2 -Wall -o /tmp/ops tools/usb/mtp-file-ops.c -lmtp && /tmp/ops`. The owner uses KDE Dolphin:`kiod6` holds the MTP interface while Dolphin has the player open — ask the owner, then end `kiod6` (it restarts on demand).
  - Player state: Internal Storage = `Old Player Files` (509 old stock files, the owner may delete them over USB now);

the owner's 138 skin files from the old Internal Storage are NOT restored yet (backup `out/deep-sleep-final/evidence/20261016/internal-storage-backup-before-stock-test/internal-music.tar`, copy on `/media/sd/.y2-backup-20261016/`). pstore still holds the 7.9 oops record.

built); restore the skins; GitHub #39 and #44 were closed 2026-10-17 with explanations. Cleanup idea: the eMMC write guard refuses ext4's WRITE_ZEROES inside p8 (`mmc_erase: group start error -30` at start, harmless). Previous entry: (2026-10-16 night) BETA 8 = FM RADIO — KICKOFF FOR A FRESH SESSION (owner: "lets start with fm radio for beta 8 but with a new fresh session"). Nothing for FM is built, planned in detail or authorised on hardware yet. The released state is Beta 7.6 (entry below).
  - Rule first (AGENTS.md): FM is a new hardware subsystem and opens the Beta 8 milestone, so the first deliverable is the roadmap/gap audit ([standing procedure](https://innioasis.app/post_install.html?url=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fschulzcode%2Fy2linux%2Fblob%2FHEAD%2Fdocs%2Fplanning%2Froadmap-gap-audit.md%23standing-milestone-boundary-rule&anchor=standing-milestone-boundary-rule)) with real hardware evidence, written as a BETA-8-FM audit row plus a plan; no build, flash or tuner power-up before the owner agrees to the plan. Device access over USB SSH (`root@10.42.0.1`, key `~/.ssh/y2linux_ed25519`, HostKeyAlias `y2linux-production-v1`) is read-only unless the owner OKs an experiment; the owner presses the buttons.
  - What is known (Android-era evidence, `../Y2Player/docs/FM_RADIO_FEASIBILITY.md`, 2026-08-01…03): the tuner is the MT6627 FM core in the CONSYS combo (chip id 0x6627), reached on stock Android through `/dev/fm` (MediaTek `fmradio` driver; vendor call sequence `opendev, powerup, tune, setmute, switchAntenna, autoscan, powerdown`). It opened, tuned and played on headphones, audio only via the HAL's indirect path (FM → I2S → AFE → DAC; the direct analog path was silent). Reception was the problem: the headphone cable is the aerial and the owner's unit received essentially nothing; register 0x61 bit 4 (antenna type) stays at the long-aerial default. §9 of that document lists the controls still owed (correct band sweep, cable/no-cable comparison, stock FM app as control, Y1/Y2 driver comparison).
  - Firmware:`mt6627_fm_v1_patch.bin`, `mt6627_fm_v1_coeff.bin` (`/etc/firmware/mt6627/`) and `/etc/fmr/mt6627_fm_cust.cfg` come from the owner's Innioasis `system.img`, like the Wi-Fi/Bluetooth firmware: never in our images; extend `tools/platform/y2_platform/radiofw.py` (Firmware drive install) to take them.
  - Where it lands in Y2Linux: our own connectivity driver `kernel/platform/connectivity/` (WMT `wmt.c`, STP `stp.c`, BTIF, firmware loader) has Wi-Fi and Bluetooth but no FM function yet; FM needs the WMT FM function-on, its STP channel and a tuner driver (donor reference: community kernel `drivers/misc/mediatek-consys/fmradio`, commit `5a0058fb`; the stock kernel ELF `out/rea/fm-kernel.elf` (local, not in git) contains the vendor FM driver: `mt6627_PowerUp`, `mt6627_DspPatch`, `mt6627_i2s_info_get`, `fm_powerup`, `fm_tune_new`, `fm_ana_switch`, 111 such strings, the reference for the exact register sequence). Audio: the AFE/I2S input path in `kernel/audio/` and the platform's audio policy. Reborn: a source next to internet radio (`reborn_platform::radio`, screens Radio/RadioStations), hidden where a scan finds nothing (owner decision 2026-10-15: the menu stays hidden on players without reception).
  - Owner decisions already made: FM menu hidden when no station is found; internet radio stays as is. Open for the plan: band/region (87.5–108 MHz, 100 kHz steps vs 50 kHz), RDS, presets, behaviour with Bluetooth output (FM audio is wired-only on stock), sleep while FM plays.
  - Paste-ready prompt for the fresh session: "Start Beta 8: FM radio. Read docs/planning/NEXT-SESSION-HANDOFF.md (START HERE), AGENTS.md and ../Y2Player/docs/FM_RADIO_FEASIBILITY.md. First do the milestone gap audit for FM with real hardware evidence (read-only on the device), then propose a phased plan (kernel FM function over WMT/STP, firmware from system.img, audio path, platform API, Reborn UI) with the open owner decisions. Do not build, flash or power the tuner until I agree."

Release `1.0.0-beta.6.3`, tag `baseline-60`, sequence 5750, Linux `b20404c` + docs, Reborn `b18d98c`; report `docs/validation/Y2-LINUX-1.0-BETA-6.3.md`, audit BETA-6.3. Everything below about 6.2 still applies; all of it is pushed.

Released on 2026-10-15/16 (all public, OTA): Beta 4 (53) internet radio → 4.1 (54) radio hang, Wi-Fi after wake, TLS clock → 5 (55) keyboard, search, UI pass → 5.1 (56) live search, Radio section → 6 (57) playlists, pause in place, seeking → 6.1 (58) Bluetooth resume (drain), skipping while a song starts, SD duplicates (#15, PR #14), review fixes → 6.2 (59) clock (PR #10), every skin updated for the newer screens, Bunny Bow skin. The owner installed Beta 6; whether 6.1/6.2 are installed is not yet known.

First thing tomorrow — device checks (ask the owner to install 6.2 if not done; device access is read-only for us, writes and experiments need the owner's go):
  - Beta 6.3: on battery, pause and press Power within a few seconds → the player sleeps; wake, Play continues at the same place (log: `pause_released``for_sleep: true`).
  - AirPods: Pause, wait, Play → sound returns (6.1 drain path; Beta 6 was silent). Log: `/data/reborn/logs/reborn-current.jsonl` (`alsa` pause events should show `device_pause: false` for `bluealsa:`).
  - Next/Previous right after starting a song (Bluetooth): no "Couldn't complete that"; Play/Pause during a start pauses on the chosen song.
  - Settings → Display → Time Zone (Berlin) → clock in Reborn's look and in two or three skins; 12/24 h follows the zone.
  - Theme → Bunny Bow: Home (cloud clock, bunny), Now Playing, lists, keyboard, a radio station (LIVE, logo, song), Station Info; skin load time in the log.
  - Any skin: radio Now Playing shows LIVE/logo/song (not "1 of 1"); Favorites heart on Now Playing.
  - Library has no second copy of SD songs after a scan (owner's DB only had 2 such rows of another card: they correctly stay).
  - Still open from earlier betas: radio after sleep/Wi-Fi wake (4.1), live search and radio logos (5.1), playlists on the device (6), AAC by ear.

Backlog on GitHub (not yet analysed): Y2Reborn PRs by samanz #11 (headphone correction from AutoEq profiles on the SD card), #12 (Settings > Sound > Measure headphones), #13 (spinners at the panel's rate) — review like #10/#14 (merge with `--no-ff` to keep the author; check against `0e14a3e`-era helpers). Y2Linux issues #39 (Firmware drive: a system.img larger than Y2DATA's free space fails silently), #40 (artist tags not displayed properly), #41 (browse by Album Artist), #42 (second Y2 drains ≈35–39 mA overnight with every sleep feature armed). Owner-parked: station logos as a Cover Flow for radio favorites; a 12/24-hour switch.

Next milestone: FM radio = Beta 7. Do the milestone-boundary audit first (`docs/planning/roadmap-gap-audit.md`, standing rule; AGENTS.md). Evidence: `../Y2Player/docs/FM_RADIO_FEASIBILITY.md`; the FM firmware must come from the owner's `system.img` like Wi-Fi/Bluetooth (no MediaTek firmware in images).

Where things are (Reborn): clock `reborn_core::clock` + `zones.rs` (regenerate with `tools/clock/zones.py`), picker/caches `reborn_ui::time_zone`; skin engine vars/flags `crates/reborn-ui/src/skin/{mod,draw,compile}.rs`; skins are generated — never edit `skins/*/skin.json` by hand: change `tools/assets/build_skins.py` (finishing steps `add_clock/add_live/add_keyboard/add_heart`; Bunny Bow in `bunny_bow()`; fonts in `assets/fonts/pixelify`) and rerun it (Classic's json is hand-written). Check that the default look stays pixel-identical by rendering `reborn-preview` before/after and diffing PNGs. Pause/BlueALSA in `crates/reborn-audio/native/audio.c` (`rb_sink_pause`); pending-start skips in `app/reborn/src/main.rs``Runtime::effect`; pre-CID cleanup `retire_pre_cid_copy` in `crates/reborn-library`.

Release mechanics (unchanged):`release.py bump --version X --sequence N` → commit both → `release.py build --label N` → verify image → docs (report, audit row, readme, state, catalog, docs README, handoff; `tools/development/check-docs.py`) → docs commit → `release.py publish` **with the GIT_AUTHOR_*/GIT_COMMITTER_* environment** (Luca, noreply address; never model attribution). Publish needs Reborn's HEAD to be the built commit: Reborn docs fixes go in after publishing. Workspace: `out/build`, `out/release-59`, `out/release-60` (prune keeps two). Next free: label 61, sequence above 5750 (Beta 7 e.g. 5800).

Previous entry: START HERE (2026-10-15 local, latest): BUILD PIPELINE REWORKED (after Beta 2; not released, not pushed). Owner: "do all of the fixes" + "never more than 2 builds on this pc".
  - Release = 3 commands (`docs/BUILDING.md` §7): `tools/production/release.py bump --version X`, commit both repos, `release.py build --label N` (≈95 s: resume 51 s, all checks in parallel 28 s, sign/firmware-scan/assets 15 s; `--clean` ≈8.5 min), docs commit, `release.py publish --label N --title … --notes … --yes` (tags, pushes, GitHub release; third-party sources archive `Y2Linux-sources-third-party-<sha12>.tar` and `linux-6.18.56.tar.xz` are linked instead of re-uploaded when an earlier release carries the identical file; writes `published.json`).
  - One build workspace `out/build`, resumed in place; build folders are never copied any more: copies kept absolute paths, the `staging` symlink and per-package RPATHs of their origin — Beta 2's build still read libraries/.pc files of `power-final-34-build`, and its `libdbus` carried build-34 paths. Outputs per release in `out/release-<N>/` (update, first-install, ota, assets, receipts, summary.json; receipts also in `out/deep-sleep-final/build<N>-receipts`).
  - Prune after every build: workspace + 2 newest release outputs (always the newest published one = next `--base`), unused `.cache/environment-*` removed; a build the workspace still references is never deleted. First run freed 55 GB; `out/` now: `build`, `deep-sleep-final` (old logs in `old-build-logs/`), `rea`, `screenshots`, `y2linux-1.0.0-beta.2-update` (base for 52).
  - Build changes: Buildroot per-package directories + ccache (`.cache/ccache`), all cores; Reborn/fdk-aac/bluez-alsa rebuilt only when their inputs change (+ reverse dependencies under PPD); `exfatprogs` now depends on util-linux (missing dependency found by the parallel build); Reborn `cross.sh` takes the package's host view (`Y2_BUILDROOT_HOST`, Reborn `bae14ca`). Clean workspace target equals Beta 2's (2310 files; only sqlite3 CLI without zlib — undeclared dependency the serial build picked up by luck —, libdbus without build-34 paths, Python sysconfig paths).
  - Tests:`tools/development/host-tests.sh` = container module list on the host, 760 OK in 45 s (needs python-pyelftools, qemu-user — installed); clang 23 warning fix in C test harnesses; new `tests/test_release_tool.py` in the suite (733 in the container).
  - Not pushed: Y2Linux commits after `4808347` and Reborn `bae14ca` are local; push needs the owner's go. Next release (52) uses the new flow; Beta 2 OTA device check still owed.

Previous entry: START HERE (2026-10-15 local, later): BETA 1 DEVICE CHECK PASSED; FIRST OVERNIGHT DRAIN ON BETA 1 ≈ 9.4 mA (archive `20261016/155-beta1-overnight-drain`, next 156). Read-only capture with `out/deep-sleep-final/capture-return.py`; no report or audit entry written yet (owner: handoff only for now).
  - Install check (open item 1, done): kernel `6.18.56-y2linux-1.0.0-beta.1`, `update status` Acknowledged (sequence 5000), `firmware status` Ready (7 files in `/data/firmware/mt6582`), recovery log without `radio firmware kept` and without a DSI `read-back` line.
  - Night 00:07–09:24 local (boot `81283b3d`; `system.log` stamps are UTC, 2 h behind): 18 timer maintenance wakes (`wake=0x1`, 30 min), vproc/PMIC sleep/wheel/USB PHY/INFRA 12/12 clean, INFRA `lost=0x0`, no taint. Morning wake `wake=0x20`.
  - Drain ≈ 9.4 mA (least squares on `suspend_maintenance_wake``remaining_uah_estimate`, first 2 h skipped; 10.8 mA with all rows; −8.8 mV/h at 4.09→4.01 V). Same script on the 26 night (archive 124): 8.3 mA at ≈3.99→3.94 V. `panel_stock_suspend`, `vgp2_idle_off` and the VSYS PMIC group were all active → 27's measures bring no measurable gain (voltage-only estimate, different SOC region: ±1–2 mA is noise). Stock ≈1.3 mA; the gap needs new sources, not a re-measurement of A–C.
  - 06:38 self-wake (known race, now in a maintenance re-entry): after the 13th maintenance wake the `/sys/power/wakeup_count` store failed (BAT0 change; `BAT0` wakeup_count 19, `mt6323-keys` 0, no key event) → `Refused('wake_event_in_progress')` → screen-first + radios restored, screen lit 63 s, Wi-Fi associated, asleep again 06:40. Code: `tools/platform/y2_platform/product_sleep.py` (store at ≈338, refusal restore at ≈380). Fix proposed since report 26 and still not made: on a failed store with unchanged Power/RTC wake IRQs, re-read the count and retry (bounded) instead of a full wake.
  - Morning radio restore: WMT patch 2 (opcode 07) BTIF timeout −110; the driver's `retrying once` succeeded 0.6 s later, Wi-Fi online. AirPods reconnect `Inhibited` again (`Host is down`, peer probably absent). USB plugged 09:25:36 → configured, charging 450 mA, SSH fine (one more good USB-after-wake point).
  - Open Reborn PRs (samanz, #9–#13; only the PR descriptions and file lists were read, code not reviewed or built, none merged; #11 and #12 conflict on one line, the hint under Output — #12 says keep its version): #9 M3U playlists, #10 status-bar clock, #11 AutoEq headphone EQ, #12 Measure headphones (Y2Linux #38 button), #13 spinners at panel rate. Owner decides.
  - Open Y2Linux issue #39 (samanz): a raw (non-sparse) `system.img` (his 3.2.1 copy, 859,826,928 B) cannot fit the Firmware drive on Y2DATA (585.8 MB free there; ours 634 MB) → MTP copy fails with ENOSPC (`mtp-events.json` error 28), nothing tells the owner. The SD card `Firmware/system.img` route worked (`candidates()` in `radiofw.py`). Our tested v3.2.0 image is sparse, 518,590,232 B. Suggested: show ENOSPC on the player, name the SD route in the inbox README (`radiofw.py``README`) and more prominently in INSTALL.md step 7, say the sparse v3.2.0 image is expected. Not answered yet.

Proposed plan (owner agreed to record it; not started, issue not answered): (3) wording first: inbox `README` in `radiofw.py` names the v3.2.0 `system.img` (≈520 MB) and the SD card `Firmware` folder for a larger copy; INSTALL.md step 7 names the SD route in the step itself. (2) Player message: `radiofw.state()` reports `{"state":"Missing","reason":"too_large"}` when the last Firmware-inbox copy failed with `error 28` in `/run/y2/mtp-events.json`; Reborn adds a hero page beside That File Isn't the Y2 Firmware, e.g. That File Doesn't Fit — use the v3.2.0 `system.img` or a `Firmware` folder on the SD card; clears on a found file or Ready. (1) Real fix, after checking upstream umtprd for an existing check: in the `y2-mtp` SendObjectInfo patch, answer `MTP_RESPONSE_STORE_FULL` when the announced object size exceeds the target storage's free space, so the computer says "not enough space" before any data moves (today STORAGE_FULL is returned only after the whole transfer, `0001-y2-safe-media-transfers.patch` ≈528, which Windows does not show clearly). Do not grow or move the Firmware drive; reading raw images already works. Order: (3)+(2) in the next release, (1) after the upstream check; samanz offered to test (has the raw 3.2.1 image). Reply on #39 only with the owner's go.
  - Next: write the 155 result into a validation report + audit entry; fix the `wake_event_in_progress` race (owner go); then continue the sleep-power gap.

Previous entry: START HERE (2026-10-16, night): 43 SEALED. The dark panel after a restart (and after power-on from charging with USB, owner's observation) is a boot-time race: Lima's runtime idle (200 ms after probe) drops the last SMI common reference before a late display handover (~0.9 s on restart boots vs ~0.47 s cold), CCF closes the gate under LK's fetch, larb0 wedges. 43 holds SMI common/larb0 until the display mutex takes over. Next: flash 43, restart + USB power-on tests, then `restart.json`.

Reborn's output was the AirPods Pro (`14:14:7D:AA:34:47`), which never connected this boot: the platform reconnect's two `ConnectProfile` calls got no reply → `/run/y2/bt-reconnect.json``Inhibited`, `completion_unknown_user_retry_required` (`tools/connectivity/reconnect.c`, unchanged since 25). Reborn: 0 connects, `pcms` empty, every play → `"connected A2DP peer required"`. The session migrated to Auto (`codec_revision` 1). 26's codec request never ran. The owner was told to connect manually; unverified whether that worked.

21:19 update (archive 122): the Y2 "woke itself" right after unplugging: no sleep happened. A `BAT0` change from the charger noticing the unplug landed during the ≈5-s radio quiesce, the `wakeup_count` store failed, and the helper refused (`wake_event_in_progress`) and restored the screen (seen twice before, both on 13). Proposed fix in the 26 report (retry the handshake with a fresh count when Power/RTC did not change); not made. AirPods reconnect failed a third time (still `Inhibited`).

Overnight baseline (archive 123, 21:24 UTC): fresh boot `8943a99a` on 26 after an owner restart, taint 0, DSI hand-over normal, SPM entries 0, sleep guard not pending, power features as wanted (`golden_apply` 15, `pmic_sleep` 7, md1/pwrap/dis on), battery 4.07 V charging on USB, the usual boot lines only (`mmc_erase` -30, thermal_zone1 -61, mt6323-led). Reconnect already `Inhibited` after one failed try (AirPods not connected). Compare the morning archive against this one.

Overnight result (archive `20261014/124-power26-overnight-result`, read 07:47 UTC): same boot `8943a99a`, taint 0. Sleep from 21:27:28 UTC to the owner's Power press at 07:46:49 UTC (10.3 h suspended): 21 kernel suspends, 20 RTC maintenance wakes (wake 0x1) + final wake 0x20 (Power), every stage result 0, no refusals, no unexplained wakes, `suspend-last``complete_result_0`, sleep guard not pending. SPM: entries/resumes 21/21, `infra_pdn_entries` 21, `golden_entries` 21, `pwrap_gates`/`ungates` 21/21 (0 failures), `md1_gate` 1 (0 regates), `dis_offs`/`ons` 23/22, aborts 0. INFRA: saves/restores 21/21, `last_lost=0x0`, `total_lost_regs` 0 (unchanged). `pmic_sleep_state` entries/restores 21/21, 0 failures. Wheel: sleeps/wakes 21/21, `wake_retries` 42 (2 NACKs per wake, same as 16's 66 for 33), 0 failures. Drain ≈8.3 mA (least squares on `remaining_uah_estimate` at maintenance wakes, first 2 h skipped; 7.6 mA without the skip; filtered voltage −5.0 mV/h, identical to 16's −5.0 mV/h / 8.7 mA by the same method) → at parity with 16, no regression from 17–26. Battery 83 % → 78 % (3.998 → 3.933 V). USB: first plug-in after the night configured at once (`udc_change` → configured, MTP+ECM Ready, SSH immediate); `dma_unmask_lost` 21 (one per sleep, re-armed, as since 17). dmesg: only the known boot lines plus the wheel NACKs. Bluetooth: AirPods still never connected this boot (`connected=false`, `pcms` empty, metrics connects/disconnects 0/0, `codec_policy` null); reconnect tried again after the morning wake and is `Inhibited` (`total_attempts` 2, `failures` 2). So items 2–3 below are still open.

Remaining-gap audit (2026-10-14, read-only, ledger F18–F27 in `docs/knowledge/stock-suspend-re-ledger.md`): the SoC rows still different at entry are checklist-only (stock never writes them). Confirmed gaps against stock: C panel suspend (stock GC9503V sends a 24-write analog power-down block after sleep-in and leaves reset high; ours holds reset low), A VGP2 always-on (stock turns the CS43131's VGP2 off when idle; sole owner), B VSYS buck never in sleep mode (stock init `VSYS_VOSEL_CTRL`/`VSLEEP_EN`/`QI_VSYS_VSLEEP`), D VEMC3V3 (known; stock `msdc_emmc_power` turns it off). Radios, SD, DRAM, GPIO: no gap found. Drain measurement is relaxation-limited (late-night 2–8 mA); the stock board has no coulomb counter. No implementation without the owner's go and a scope-audit entry.

Codec row Auto/SBC/AAC in Settings.

Rules (unchanged): read-only first, archive before anything; live register writes only with the owner's OK (a loaded probe taints the kernel → sleep refused until a hold-Power restart); scope-audit entry before source changes; after editing a kernel overlay patch use `tools/build/refresh-overlays.py` (manifest hash); builds: `build.py --output out/power-final-NN-build --owner-firmware out/deep-sleep-final/owner-firmware`, `system_update.py --build … --base out/y2linux-platform-v1-power-final-candidate-24 --fallback-rootfs …/Y2ROOT.img --output …`, then validate/check_elf/check_installed_arm/privacy/release_inventory and the container suite (`tools/build/run.py --output B -- sh /project/tools/production/tests.sh`); Reborn: `cargo fmt`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace`, GLES check `tools/preview/gles_compare.py` on `reborn-preview` output; commit with the Luca identity (env vars), no attribution.

PMIC_WRAP live test DONE (2026-10-13 13:35, owner-authorised, ledger F17): gating INFRA bit 23 makes the AP's PMIC wrapper read as all-zero (WACS2 read -EIO, no hang); ungating restores it at once. So bit 23 is the AP-side wrapper clock and can only be gated after the last AP PMIC access in `y2_spm_enter` and must be ungated before the first restore. The probe tainted boot `69ef8c26` (taint 4096): sleep is refused until a hold-Power restart.

PMIC_WRAP investigation (2026-10-13, REA/static, ledger F17): INFRA bit 23 (PMIC_WRAP clock) is the only INFRA gate still differing at sleep entry. Stock checklists gate it in suspend and even awake in video playback/3G paging, so AP PMIC access does not need it; stock never gates it by clock ID (indirect). Our keep-on reason (SPM firmware uses the wrapper) is not supported by stock. Next (after 18's overnight, because a probe taints the kernel and blocks sleep): owner-authorised live probe: INFRA_PDN_SET (0x10001040) bit 23 → read a PMIC register through the wrapper (e.g. `cat /sys/module/pwrap/parameters/cpu_voltage_uv`) and `PWRAP` state → INFRA_PDN_CLR (0x10001044) bit 23; then hold-Power restart. If AP access survives: 19 = gate bit 23 as the very last write before the PCM kick and ungate as the very first after the wake (before the PMIC/VPROC restores), switch `golden_apply` bit 16; then read `last_lost`.

Step 1 — read 16's night. The owner charged to 86 % (stock curve), unplugged and started the night on 2026-10-12 evening. Check first that the kernel was not tainted: the live MD1 probe (`md1probe.ko`) tainted boot `0b0e8a77`, and product sleep refuses on a tainted kernel; if the owner did not hold-Power restart before unplugging, the rows show `suspend_refused_kernel_sleep_unavailable` all night. Archive read-only first (`109` layout: `first-return.json`, `persistent.tar` of `/data/system/platform`, `/data/logs`, `/run/y2`), next number `110`. Read `sleep-diagnostics.jsonl` (`kernel_resumed` rows carry `golden` with `extra` rows and `pmic_golden`) and `power-journal.jsonl`. Drain: skip the first 1–2 h (post-charge relaxation; 16's 36-min test fell 4033 → 3935 mV from relaxation alone), then use resting voltages at the maintenance wakes on the stock curve (`battery-profile.json`, 1300 mAh). Compare with 15 ≈10 mA (archive 108, re-read on the stock curve). USB after the morning replug (Defect B): 16's first wake worked, but the DMA unmask was not lost (`dma_intr_before=0000ff00`, bits 15..8 are the readback), so the cause of the fix is unproven; record every wake.

17 work list (in this order, adjusted by the night):
  - Gate the never-used modem's clock requests at boot (PERICFG MD1_SET 0x20 = 0x3ffffff; proven AP-writable and harmless by the live test, ledger F11), with a switch in `power_features` and a boot-time readback of MD1_STA 0x30. Main suspect for INFRA never powering down (`last_lost=0x0` on every 16 sleep).
  - Already fixed in source (d1953fa), only needs the build: the MMSYS DCM call (0011 overlay hash) and the DMA unmask readback field.
  - INFRA power-down: after 1, read `y2_infra/state``last_lost`; if still 0, find the remaining requester (SRCLKENA, PCM R13 `0xe8060000` bits, MD_MASK 0x38 = 3).
  - PMIC golden: 15/44 rows differ (0x04e, 0x102, 0x108, 0x10e, 0x120, 0x212, 0x216, 0x21c, 0x230, 0x256, 0x310, 0x508, 0x70c, 0x776, 0x778). Map each to its regulator (MT6323), adopt the stock suspend value with save/restore around sleep, like `golden-apply-policy.h`. Do not touch AUXADC trims (battery readings).
  - Remaining SoC golden rows (11): bus DCM at entry (TOPCKGEN+0x004, stock `bus_dcm_enable` writes 0x8f), CLK_CFG_4, AP_PLL_CON1/CON2 (decode first), MAINPLL/AXI only if the drain demands it. Deliberate exceptions stay: console UART mux and gate, PMIC_WRAP, PERI DCMDBC (Y2 stock code).
  - Other stock suspend hooks: panel deep standby (`lcm_suspend` 0xc04a24f4 table), VEMC3V3 off and the VGP2 DAC rail in suspend, `pmic_early_suspend` (VREF18/AUXADC), PMIC BAT_L interrupt instead of the 30-min timer wake.
  - Faster wake (owner request 2026-10-12, no deep-sleep trade-off): the screen stays dark 2–3 s after Power. 16's 16:08 wake: `kernel_resumed` 16:08:33 → `restoring_radios` 16:08:33 → `complete_result_0` 16:08:35, so the SoC is back in ≲1 s and most of the dark time waits for the Wi-Fi/Bluetooth restore before the UI gets the screen. First measure the morning wake with millisecond kernel-log timestamps (PM resume, SPM exit, device resume, DIS power-on/DSI modeset, panel, backlight, radio restore). Then, ordered by expected gain: (a) screen first, radios restored in the background (≈1.5–2 s; stock Android does this), (b) backlight as soon as the pipeline is up with Reborn's last frame, (c) panel deep standby (`lcm_suspend`, also item 6), (d) DIS domain costs only milliseconds. Works → Defect B is sleep-specific (supports the lost-unmask theory); fails → it is the stage 5 → 6 reconnect path itself (compare `y2_usb_reconnect()` vs `y2_usb_register()`, Track A.2).

Track A — resume 100 %.
  - Defect B (USB dead after every wake), root-cause lead. 14's records (archive 106): after the wake USB is `configured`, usb0 up with its address, MTP/SSH running; RX keeps counting (PIO), TX is frozen; the controller programmed transmit DMA (`dma_tx_programs` − `dma_irqs` = 2) whose completion interrupt never came; `dma_aborts` 6 → 12. Receive uses PIO (`rx_dma=0`), transmit uses Inventra DMA. Hypothesis: the MUSB DMA-interrupt unmask (register `MUSB base + 0x200`, bits 24..31) is written exactly once, in `y2_musb_dma` creation (`kernel/platform/usb.c`: `writel(0xff0000ff, base + 0x200)`; upstream `drivers/usb/musb/mediatek.c` also writes `DMA_INTR_STATUS_MSK | DMA_INTR_UNMASK_SET_MSK` once at init). If the MAC loses it in SPM sleep, DMA completions stay masked forever. `y2_musb_enable` rewrites L1INTM (`15` with DMA) on reconnect but never 0x200; `y2_musb_clear_stale` only W1C-clears the low byte. Verify read-only first: add `dma_intr32` (full 32-bit read of 0x200, plus the eight channel CNTL/ADDR/COUNT at 0x204+16n, L1INTS/L1INTM 0xa0/0xa4 and TXCSR of the ECM/MTP IN endpoints) to the y2-usb `status` attribute and to `sleep_diagnostics.usb_net`; expect bits 24..31 set before sleep and 0 after the wake. Fix (16): when `y2_usb_dma_active`, re-write the unmask (`0xff000000`, plus W1C of the sampled low byte) in `y2_musb_enable()` and/or `y2_musb_after_restore()`, with a host test in the style of `tests/test_usb_*`. Fallback experiment only with owner consent (a live sysfs write): one night with `echo 0 > /sys/bus/platform/drivers/y2-usb/*/phy_savecurrent` to rule out 13's PHY save-current. Note 12 showed Defect B once before PHY save-current existed.
  - After the wake the reconnect ran only stages 5 → 6 (first plug-in after boot runs 1 → 6). Check `y2_usb_reconnect()` against `y2_usb_register()`: anything the full path programs that SPM sleep can reset (DMA unmask above, FIFO layout is rechecked in `musb_start`).
  - INFRA never really powers down: every 14 restore says `lost=0x0`, `pdn0 0x2fcfbfd->0x2fcfbfd`. The stock state needs INFRA off. Check the SPM PCM flags / `CC_DISABLE_INFRA_PWR`, `PWR_STATUS` bit 6 during sleep, and what the stock `slp_infra_pdn` path does that we don't (`out/deep-sleep-final/vendor-suspend-source/` is gone; re-extract from `evidence-private/20260908-ram-map/fm-decompressed.bin` + `fm-symbols.json`).
  - Still open: software Restart leaves the panel dark (11; never investigated — do not software-restart); new on 15: `Exception ignored while flushing sys.stdout: OSError EINVAL` from `y2-platform boot-stage` (S03y2-platform) and `cpu-idle-policy` (S05y2-cpu-idle), whose output goes to `/dev/kmsg` (Python 3.15; only log text lost; fix by line-sized writes or logging through syslog); panel logs `Skipping unprepare of already unprepared panel` at every screen-off on 15 (wake works; check whether DRM now disables the pipeline at screen-off, which would also be the awake-dark power win).

Track B — complete the golden master. Source: the Y2's own stock FM kernel (`evidence-private/20260908-ram-map/fm-decompressed.bin`, base 0xc0008000, `fm-symbols.json`); data `docs/validation/data/y2-stock-suspend-golden-20261011.json`; capture `cat /sys/devices/platform/10006000.power-controller/golden` (also stored in each `kernel_resumed` row). 14 at sleep entry: 26 of 34 SoC rows differ (value&mask vs stock):
  - Clock muxes: TOPCKGEN+0x004 (e=0, stock 0x80), CLK_CFG_0..4 (mux power-down bits not set at entry; stock sets them).
  - PLLs on at entry (stock: off, hardware-controlled): MAINPLL_CON0 0x79000101, UNIVPLL_CON0 0xfd000001, MMPLL_CON0 0x111, MSDCPLL_CON0 0x121, MSDCPLL_PWR_CON0 0x80000001 (stock 2 = off + isolated), VENCPLL_CON0 0xa121 (stock off); AP_PLL_CON1 0xcc01c0 (stock 0), AP_PLL_CON2 4 (stock 0).
  - INFRA/PERI clock gates and DCM: INFRA_PDN_STA 0xb0a0 vs stock 0x80bfe6 (most INFRA clocks still ungated), INFRACFG+0x010/0x014/0x050/0x054/0x058 (bus DCM), PERI_PDN0_STA 0x2fcfbfd vs 0x2feffff, PERI_PDN0_MD1_STA 0 vs all-gated, PERICFG+0x050/0x054 (peri DCM).
  - DRAMC+0x1dc (self-refresh/power-down bits), MCUSYS+0x05c.

Already matching: CLK_SCP_CFG_0/1, AP_PLL_CON3 hardware-control bits (0x780000), TOPCKGEN+0x00c, INFRACFG+0x008. Plan: rank by risk; implement one group per candidate with a run-time switch and an A/B night each, read-only capture before/after. Lowest risk: INFRA/PERI clock gates of unused blocks and PERI MD1 gating; then bus/peri DCM; then CLK_CFG mux power-down at entry; then PLL software-off at entry (MSDC/MM/VENC first, MAIN/UNIV last, they feed the CPU/bus) with hardware control; DRAMC last. PMIC golden (44 rows, `mt6323_power_gs_suspend_data`; 55 of 80 stock init fields differed on 12): re-run the read-only PMIC comparison on 15 (`tools/development/power-session/suspend-probe.py`, `arm-suspend-probe.py`, `tools/development/analyze-suspend-probe.py` — arming writes a probe on the device: owner consent first). Stock hooks not yet adopted: VEMC3V3 off in suspend, VGP2/DAC rail, panel deep standby (`lcm_suspend`), AUXADC hardware mode + VREF18 off, PMIC BAT_L interrupt instead of timer wakes.

Data: `docs/validation/data/y2-stock-suspend-golden-20261011.json` (13 branch). 13 changes (all run-time switchable): radio quiesce retry + guard only after kernel entry; VPROC 0.85 V sleep voltage inside `->enter()` only (`/sys/module/pwrap/parameters/vproc_sleep`, `vproc_sleep_state`); LP_SEL on VMC/VMCH/VEMC3V3 (`/sys/firmware/y2_pmic/lp_sel`); USB PHY save-current in noirq (`y2-usb/*/phy_savecurrent`); wheel sleep/reset (`/sys/bus/i2c/devices/*-0051/sleep`); maintenance wake 1800 s; read-only `golden` on `10006000.power-controller`. After the owner flashes (read-only first): identity/taint; wheel, USB, BT work; then one unplugged sleep ≥ 35 min and read: `vproc_sleep_state` (entries = restores, failures 0), `phy_savecurrent` (saves = restores, mismatches 0), wheel `sleep` (sleeps = wakes), `y2_infra/state` (`restores>0`?), `golden` (which stock SoC rows still differ: PLL software enables, AP_PLL_CON3 hardware control, CLK_SCP_CFG, INFRA/PERI gates). Then an overnight drain vs 12's ≈2.2 points/h. Next candidate (14) is decided from `golden`: most likely PLL hardware control (the stock table expects MAIN/UNIV/MM/MSDC PLL software enable 0 at sleep entry) and the DIS/MMSYS domain. Not done: eMMC supply removal, VGP2, panel deep standby, PMIC BAT_L interrupt, awake-dark display pipeline (Reborn blanks only the backlight). Never software-restart (dead panel); no live register writes without consent.

OVERNIGHT RESULT ON POWER-FINAL-12 (2026-10-11 morning) — START HERE. Archive (read-only copies): `out/deep-sleep-final/evidence/20261011/97-power12-overnight-return/` (`power-journal.jsonl`, `power-journal.previous.jsonl`, `suspend-last.json`, `system.log.0`; the night's syslog was NOT persisted past 23:46, pstore was empty after the hold-Power reset). Night boot `11f3bfa9…`; the owner restarted at the end, now boot `f3b6f6d3…` (kernel -12, taint 0, `pm_async` 0, no sleep since). Device clock = host UTC minus about 33 min. Worked: synchronous device suspend. 34 kernel suspends 23:48-05:19 (device time), 33 clean 10-min maintenance wakes, suspended 99.6% of the time, no stall, no reset. Drain 91% to 79% in 5.5 h, about 2.2 points/h (4.074 to 3.995 V), the same as 05 (2.1-2.2), so `infra_pdn=1` (06) and `LP_SEL` (05) did not visibly lower the floor. One night only. Defect A (candidate 13): owner woke it with Power at 05:26:48 (`wake_reason=power`); a second sleep request 9 s later failed in `y2-radio ... off-runtime` (`sleep_service_failed:y2-radio`, product_failure within 1 s). Suspect `y2-platform bluetooth-power off` (`tools/platform/y2_platform/bt_control.py`: returns `platform_bluetooth_operation_in_progress` if the lock is held, else `bluetoothctl power off`) racing the Bluetooth `on-runtime` restore of the previous wake. The first sleep at 23:48 with the same radio state worked. Because the kernel was never entered the result is 1 and `product_sleep.py` (end of the run function) leaves the guard pending, so ALL sleep is refused (`sleep_recovery_required`, 80 refusals) until reboot; the device stayed awake with a dark screen 05:27-06:53 (about 6.7 points/h, 78% to 69%). Fix ideas: serialize/retry the radio quiesce with the BT lock and a settle wait, and clear the guard when `kernel_completed` is false and the radios were restored. Defect B: in that stuck state the USB gadget re-enumerated on cable reconnect (0525:a4aa, host interface up) but the host received 0 bytes, no ping or SSH, until the owner restarted. Cause not investigated (suspend-last.json `usb_restore`: `configured=0 monitor_parked=1 detached=1`, udc "not attached"). Open question: the owner reports the UI showed 100% instead of about 69%. The platform journal never exceeded 98% after unplug and Reborn displays `platform.battery.percent` as given (`Y2Reborn/crates/reborn-platform/src/power.rs`), so the cause is unknown. Ask the owner WHERE and WHEN (status icon, battery screen, right after waking, after replugging) before guessing; check whether Reborn keeps a stale value when the status record is older than 5 s (`fresh_record`). Is 2.2 points/h good? No, it is high (assessment, not measured: general experience says a few mA deep-sleep floor, roughly 0.1-0.5 points/h; 2.2 points/h is about 45 h standby). Capacity is unknown and there is no current sensor, so no mA claim. The voltage-derived percent is unreliable on the flat part of the curve. Golden PMIC table (vendor `mt6323_power_gs_suspend_data`) is still mostly open. Data on the 05 branch: `git show codex/platform-v1-power-final-05:docs/validation/data/y2-pmic-suspend-golden-vs-awake-20261008.json` and `docs/validation/Y2-PLATFORM-V1-SUSPEND-POWER-OPTIMIZATION.md` (§21 ranking). 17 differences at suspend entry on 05 (22 before `LP_SEL`); no PMIC commit exists between 05 and 12. Not done: `LP_SEL` on VMC/VMCH/VEMC3V3 (blocked earlier by the safety classifier), VEMC3V3 off in suspend (high risk, patch 0029 retains it), VPROC/VSYS sleep-voltage enables (`0x212 0x216 0x21c 0x230 0x256 0x310`, conflicts with software DVFS), VUSB still on with the cable out, VGP2 on, clock/OSC bits `0x776/0x778`. Done: `LP_SEL` on VA/VIO28/VUSB/VM/VIO18, vendor 597-word `pcm_suspend` (byte-identical). `infra_pdn=1` is armed (`/sys/firmware/y2_infra/mode` = powerdown) but NO evidence yet that INFRA really loses power. The suspend-entry comparison was NOT re-run on 12. Plan for the next session, in order:
  - (Read-only, no flash) Owner unplugs, screen times out, leave 15-20 min (do not press Power twice within seconds of a wake: that triggers Defect A). Then read `/sys/firmware/y2_infra/state`: `restores>0` and `last_lost` non-zero means INFRA really powers down; `restores=0` after real sleeps means it never does (explains the unchanged floor). Take the device lease `flock -n /tmp/y2-platform-v1-device.lock`, ssh helper: `ssh -F /dev/null -o HostKeyAlias=y2linux-production-v1 -i ~/.ssh/y2linux_ed25519 root@10.42.0.1`.
  - Re-run the suspend-entry PMIC comparison on 12 with the 05-branch tools `tools/development/power-session/{suspend-probe,arm-suspend-probe}.py` and `tools/development/analyze-suspend-probe.py`. This writes on the device (arms a probe): say what will run and get the owner's go-ahead first.
  - Get a real number: an inline µA-resolution meter (PPK2 class) on the battery lead; the top-up integral from a USB meter is only an approximation.
  - Candidate 13 = Defect A fix (+ Defect B investigation), then the low-risk golden items one at a time (VMC/VMCH `LP_SEL`, VUSB), high-risk ones (VEMC3V3, VPROC/VSYS) only with an A/B plan and owner approval. Never software-restart (dead panel finding), no live register writes without consent. Nothing in this entry is committed.

Expect the percentage to slide from 100 toward the curve (~94% at 4.09 V) at 1%/min after unplugging; the Full latch only holds while the charger reports Full.

**Previous: 2026-10-08 late local analysis — device-phase stall diagnostic prepared (source only; nothing built, flashed, deployed or run on hardware).** Reconciling archives 65/66 with the exact03 suspend/device PM code gives these findings:
  - Chronology. Archive65's host capture (03:12:20 CEST, BOOTTIME 182 s) puts

boot `0132fb21` start near 03:09:18 CEST, so the kernel suspend request (BOOTTIME 398.4 s) fell near 03:15:57. Archive66's first SSH (07:44:11 UTC, BOOTTIME 23261.9 s) puts boot `81dce40c` start near 03:16:29 CEST. The unplanned reset therefore came roughly half a minute after the last callback, not hours later. That is consistent with the 30 s RGU backstop the product helper arms before every entry (`product_sleep.py`), within host-timestamp error of several seconds. It does not prove the reset source (RGU status is raw0 even for known backstop resets). The ring header `backstop_s=0` only reflects that the helper's HELPER_REQUEST mark resets the ring before the backstop is written; it is not evidence that no backstop was armed.
  - What the ring can and cannot say.`y2_pm_device()` is called only from

`dpm_run_callback()`, so it records devices that have a PM callback. Every logged entry has its leave. The stall is therefore in a step the ring cannot see: `dpm_wait_for_subordinate()`, `pm_runtime_barrier()`, `device_lock()`, the silent post-callback bookkeeping, `dpm_list_mtx`, or the global `async_synchronize_full()` at the end of `dpm_suspend()` (Linux 6.18's new asynchronous scheme). A bus hang inside a runtime-resume triggered by the barrier is also possible and would be silent. Every failure so far (clean02 archive28b, 404ef43c core, 03 boot 0132fb21) ends at the same `11008000.i2c` leave; the registration order after it is not yet known.
  - No known upstream cause. Stable 6.18.y PM-core fixes after 6.18.0

(`f384497`, `c9670fd`, `bc6fdd4`) concern the late/early runtime-PM enable order, a no_pm completion token and prepare-error cleanup. None describes a device-phase wait, so no backport is justified by the evidence.
  - Prepared, unbuilt diagnostic (smallest that separates the hypotheses).

Overlay `0059` now also records `WAIT` (before the child/consumer wait and runtime barrier) and `LOCK` (around `device_lock`) ring entries per device and logs legacy-bus suspend. `pm-journal.c` gains a built-in sampler armed by a successful `DPM_PREPARED`: at 4 s and 12 s without `DEVICES_SUSPENDED` it writes the suspender's wchan and up to three D-state tasks to the retained ring and full stacks (suspender, blocked tasks, busy workqueues) to the RAM console with only that console un-suspended. No external module: no taint. A hard bus hang that stops the worker is itself distinguishable (ring ends at the last `WAIT`/`LOCK` entry with no `dpm_stall` note). Regression: `tests/test_dpm_stall_diagnostics.py` (7 cases); fix01-03, platform suspend, CPU PM, product auto-sleep and sleep-product suites still pass (102 cases); the three changed objects cross-compile warning-free under the production config in the locked sandbox (`out/dpm-stall-compile-check/`, new directory).
  - Not claimed. This is diagnosis tooling, not a fix. pstore/DRAM retention

across the RGU reset is unproven for the console sink (the SRAM ring is the authoritative sink). Nothing here changes the pending 03 guard or any evidence.

All eleven `codex/platform-v1-<name>` branches plus `codex/highres-audio-completion` are merged into `main` in both repos, with POWER-FINAL-03 as the baseline: on every collision 03's version wins, docs are unioned, nothing from 03 was overwritten.

How it was verified (repeat it if in doubt): for each branch, every code line the branch added relative to its fork point was searched in the integrated tree (`lost-lines` check). Result: the branches were already integrated into 03's lineage in adapted form by an earlier integration pass, so almost all of their code is present. Reborn code differs from 03 only by the new probe file. The sealed 03 images therefore still correspond to `main`.

Final gates on the integrated trees: Linux production profile 515 cases (511 plus 4 new), 10 skips, 0 failures; Reborn fmt, strict clippy and all tests pass with the same counts as 03.

Per-branch outcome (Linux code that 03 already had in a newer form was kept; "absent" = branch lines not in the result, all superseded variants):````````

| Branch | Result |
| --- | --- |
| storage, wifi, bluetooth, highres-audio, suspend, input | code already in 03; docs added. Absent lines: 1-15 per branch (old wait() signature, bt-control.ini location, patch hunk headers) |
| usb | superseded by USB02: 36 of 2079 lines absent, all config/mechanics 03 changed after hardware validation. Kept 03's usb.py, mtp-safe.*, patch 0065, config |

Correction to an earlier statement in this file's previous version: it said all 12 Reborn branches "merge cleanly". That was wrong: the Reborn repo has no Git identity, so those trial merges aborted and the result was an artefact. The real Reborn merges (with identity from environment variables) had code conflicts in `main.rs`, `input.rs`, `storage.rs`, `media.c`, the library and others. They were resolved toward 03 after checking that 03 already contains each feature in adapted form (`TransferRefresh`/USB-triggered refresh, `input_resume`/`resumed()`, `pinned_folder_artwork`, `artwork_lowres`, `metadata_revision`, a superset `main_loop_interval`). Three merges (ffmpeg-metadata, reborn-performance, input) had auto-merged partial hunks that did not compile against 03 (an `evdev_us` field without producers, a duplicate `metadata_tests` module); those files were restored to 03 in a visible commit.

Not taken on purpose, because 03 does it differently and was hardware-validated or is the Product UI v2 policy: the usb branch's thread-based service wake, its gadget `MaxPower`/storage-line mechanics, the input branch's Power key mapping to screen-sleep, reborn-performance's `evdev_us` input timestamps and simple loop interval. If the owner wants any of them, port that single feature deliberately and re-run the gates. One optional small port: the usb branch's `tools/usb/qualify.py` takes `/tmp/y2-platform-v1-device.lock` (03's does not).

Branches are now merged, so cleanup may delete them:`git branch --merged main` includes all of these in both repos. Keep the archive tags rule for anything still unmerged. The earlier warning about not deleting these branches no longer applies.

## Job 1 — hardware validation of 04 (after the owner flashes)

Take `/tmp/y2-platform-v1-device.lock` first (`flock -n … sleep N &`; release by killing that PID, not `pkill -f`). The classifier asks for explicit user consent before launching scripts that write on the device: say what will run, then run it.
  - Identity:`/etc/y2linux/versions.json` = release `…candidate.4`, build `…POWER-FINAL-04`, kernel `…power-final-04`, Linux

`5aeba051…`, Reborn `8cd365da…`; boot id, taint 0. The live symlink `/usr/bin/y2-platform` disappears with the new root: after the flash `ls /usr/bin/y2-platform` must fail while Power still works, which proves the Reborn path fix.
  - Smoke gate, DAC, supervisor: repeat `power-session/dac-readback.sh` and `proc-rates.sh` (they passed on 02).
  - Suspend (the main goal): run `power-session/suspend-session.py` (README). After the recovery boot read:

`cat /sys/firmware/y2_pm/state /sys/firmware/y2_pm/devices`; look for `thaw_stall`, `S:<comm>`, `D:<comm>`, `thaw_blocked`, `I:…mmc` entries. Each `S:`/`D:` entry's number is a kernel address: resolve it with `awk` on `out/y2linux-platform-v1-power-final-candidate-04-symbols/System.map` (use the map that matches the running kernel; 03's map is not interchangeable) (nearest lower symbol). Then `mount -t pstore pstore /sys/fs/pstore; ls /sys/fs/pstore` — a `console-ramoops*` file means DRAM survived and gives the full dmesg of the stalled boot (enable `/sys/power/pm_print_times` and `pm_debug_messages` first). `oops_pc`/`oops_lr`/`panic` entries mean a crash, not a hang. Hypothesis to confirm or kill: storage I/O blocked after reset-return (`filesystems_thaw` waiting on `s_umount` while a task is stuck in `mmc_wait_for_req`), eMMC controller state not restored. Fix the cause, do not skip thaw. Backstop maximum is 30 s.
  - Wi-Fi idle policy: battery, dark, Wi-Fi on: after about five minutes C3 entries must appear (`cpuidle/state2/usage`), Reborn event log

`idle_power_down`; screen wake must restore Wi-Fi without touching `/data/network/enabled`.
  - Remaining matrix: Bluetooth connected idle vs C3, missing Bluetooth peer (owner switches headphones off), Power then RTC wake cycles

(5, then 10) once a same-boot resume exists, PC and wall charging and source transitions (ask the owner to connect the wall charger), 5/30/120-minute idle, then an overnight procedure. Report programmed ceilings, never measured mA.
  - If kernel or root changes are needed: accumulate fixes and build one`POWER-FINAL-05` (see

`~/.claude/projects/…/memory/y2-power-candidate-build-lessons.md` for the exact build, package, validate and seal commands and the `.cache` pitfalls). Never flash, never push.

## Facts that are easy to get wrong
  - Backstop range is 10..30 s. A reset is a failed suspend, never a resume.
  - C3 is gated by an active charger, so any window with USB attached shows no C2/C3.
  - Associated Wi-Fi blocks C2/C3 (APDMA bit 11, BTIF bit 20); radios-off battery windows give about 74% C3.
  - Voltage slopes mix relaxation with load. There is no current sensor.
  - The idle-bias fix means the codec is already down at suspend, so the old regcache stall cannot recur; the new stall is later.
  - Tests run in the project container (`python3 tools/build/run.py --output <build> -- sh /project/tools/production/tests.sh`); the host lacks

`qemu-arm` and `pyelftools`, so about 58 host-suite errors are environmental.
  - Commit with `GIT_AUTHOR_*`/`GIT_COMMITTER_*` = Luca `116190857+SchulzCode@users.noreply.github.com`; never change git config, never add attribution.

These are Reborn (Y2Linux)'s own instructions, shown here word for word.
